<h3><a href="https://x.com/dean_mcpherson/status/2102608002188722507">Jev-style decision model: JSON Schema instead of a question list</a></h3>
<p>The model takes a JSON Schema instead of a question list, scores every independent field in one pass while dependent fields wait, and generation is per-field opt-in so the default never generates a token.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>该模型接收 JSON Schema 而非问题列表，单次遍历为每个独立字段评分，依赖字段等待处理；生成按字段选择加入，默认不生成 token。</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/Flylocus/status/2102574294383640628">Jev Exposes an Agent Engineering Pitfall: Don&#39;t Use Reasoning Models for Low-Entropy Decisions</a></h3>
<p>The post argues that many teams wrongly assume swapping in the latest LLM will stabilize their agents, while failures often come from using 5-second-plus reasoning models for low-entropy Yes/No decisions, and sees tools like Jev as valuable for exposing this engineering issue.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>帖子指出许多团队误以为换用最新 LLM 就能让 Agent 稳定，实际事故常源于用耗时 5 秒以上的推理模型处理 Yes/No 这类低熵判断，并认为 Jev 这类工具的价值在于揭示这一工程问题。</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/Flylocus/status/2102574771888345130">Three-Layer Agent Architecture: Jev as the Decision Layer</a></h3>
<p>The post argues that mature agent architectures will move from a single large model to three layers: the LLM for reasoning and planning, lightweight gates like Jev for routing, intent classification and guardrails, and code/regex for deterministic state machines and schemas.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>帖子提出成熟 Agent 架构将从单一模型走向三层分工：大模型负责推理规划，Jev 等轻量判门负责路由、意图归类与 Guardrail，代码/正则负责确定性状态机与 Schema。</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/EJZhang_TUKEY/status/2102577882987921893">Jev Is Not a Chatbot: Non-Autoregressive Design for Fast Structured Decisions</a></h3>
<p>Jev doesn&#39;t write paragraphs, code, or chain-of-thought explanations. It&#39;s built for fast, structured decisions: routing requests, scoring risk, choosing options, answering yes or no. Its non-autoregressive design is key to this.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>Jev 不写段落、代码或思维链解释，而是为快速结构化决策而构建：路由请求、风险评分、选择选项、回答是或否。其非自回归设计是实现这一目标的关键。</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/thelazyyogii/status/2102580258687926516">Jev vs Laya: Decisive Judgment Agents That Don&#39;t Chat</a></h3>
<p>The post compares Jev and Laya: both skip chit-chat and only decide pass/fail or category. Jev is like an external judge, ready out of the box with pay-per-use pricing, more stable with many options or long inputs; Laya is like an in-house judge.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>帖子对比 Jev 与 Laya：两者都不陪聊，只做「过不过 / 归哪类」的判定。Jev 像外部裁判，开箱即用、按量付费，选项多或材料长时更稳；Laya 则像自建厨房里的裁判。</p>

<p>JEV is an AI model from TypeSafe that helps software make decisions: you provide information, ask a specific question, and define acceptable answer types, and it returns a structured result your code can use directly.</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/theanandprasad/status/2102199067057307900">Turn Open-Source LLMs into Jev-like Fast Decision Models</a></h3>
<p>Explains how to turn low-cost open-source LLMs into Jev-like fast decision models without training, and how LLMs score vocabulary to pick the next word.</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/hackerlogs/status/2102251066582573337">Jev&#39;s typed System One gate: zero-hallucination means schema match, not correct deny</a></h3>
<p>TypeSafe AI shipped Jev on 15 Sep as a typed System One gate: state in, Choice/Score/yes-no out, 70 to 500ms, no free-form string. The post notes Jev cannot invent a tool but can still pick the wrong one, and zero-hallucination means schema match, not a correct deny.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>TypeSafe AI 于 9 月 15 日发布 Jev，作为类型化的 System One 门控：输入状态，输出 Choice/Score/yes-no，延迟 70 至 500 毫秒，不支持自由文本。帖子指出 Jev 无法发明工具，但可能选错工具，零幻觉仅指模式匹配而非正确拒绝。</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/JunaidBurke/status/2102412348447166739">Jev Model: Returns Structured Decisions Instead of Generating Text</a></h3>
<p>Jev is a different kind of AI model: given a situation and typed questions, it returns structured decisions — Choice, Score, or probability — instead of generating prose. This makes it especially interesting for AI agents like tool routing and scoring.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>Jev 是一种不同的 AI 模型：给定情境和类型化问题，它不生成散文，而是返回结构化决策——选择、评分或概率。这使其特别适合 AI 智能体，如工具路由和评分。</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/NickLo641579/status/2101794549135716518">Kev-0.6B Jev-class decision model runs one forward pass on Apple Neural Engine</a></h3>
<p>Kev-0.6B is a Jev-class decision model running on the Apple Neural Engine. One forward pass, no token generation, 7.18 ms per decision with p99 of 7.53 ms. <h3><a href="https://x.com/dnzxlyfe/status/2101698685583409243">Jev&#39;s slot: inner-loop judgment, not a second brain</a></h3>
<p>Jev&#39;s slot is not a second brain but inner-loop judgment: state in, typed answers out, code owns the rest. LLM still writes, Jev branches; schema-closed does not mean correct—a wrong in-schema pick still ships if you skip the threshold.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>Jev 的 slot 不是第二大脑，而是内循环判断：输入状态，输出类型化答案，其余交给代码。LLM 仍负责编写，Jev 负责分支；schema 闭合不等于正确，跳过阈值仍可能发布错误选择。</p>

<h3><a href="https://x.com/arith_rose/status/2101278824046682230">Experimenting with TypeSafe AI&#39;s Jev in Playground: Testing Semantic Evaluation via HEX/RGB</a></h3>
<p>Testing Jev in Playground: hiding Choice candidate names as option_a etc. and providing only HEX/RGB values to see if the model evaluates them semantically as colors. <h3><a href="https://x.com/atulit_gaur/status/2100485253395882023">Jev Model: How State and Typed Questions Work</a></h3>
<p>Jev is not a chatbot, agent, or paragraph-generating model; it takes a state plus typed questions (Choice, Score, Noul) and returns an answer, probability distribution, and confidence.</p>
<p><strong>中文摘要：</strong>Jev 不是聊天机器人、智能体或生成段落的大模型；它接收状态和类型化问题（Choice、Score、Noul），返回答案、概率分布和置信度。</p>
